# you asked

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
SHA-256 TOPOLOGICAL MAPPER — Batch Experiment (Next Run)

Goal
----
Move from "one message demo" to an empirical result.

For each message in a batch:
  1) Build SHA-derived chain (T1 -> isotropic 3D mapping)
  2) Compute metrics (Rg, Ree, r_rw, r_ee, contact_density)
  3) Compare to an isotropic random-walk null model (RW_SAMPLES)
  4) Record empirical p-values for closure (Ree small) and r_ee large
Then summarize:
  - fraction of messages with p(Ree) <= 0.01 (should be ~1% under null)
  - percentiles distribution
  - show top-K most closed messages
Optionally export PDBs for top-K.

Scope
-----
Single 512-bit block only (64 rounds) by design.

No external dependencies. No truncation of outputs (prints full lists).

"""

import math
import struct
import random
import os
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict

# =========================
# CONFIG (EDIT THESE)
# =========================

BOND_LENGTH = 3.8
CONTACT_CUTOFF = 8.0
MIN_SEQ_SEP = 4

# Batch size: how many messages to test
BATCH_MESSAGES = 200

# Message generation mode: "random_bytes" or "words"
MODE = "random_bytes"

# For MODE="random_bytes"
RANDOM_MSG_LEN = 8

# Null model samples per message (increase for tighter p-values)
RW_SAMPLES = 1000

# Reproducibility
SEED_MESSAGES = 123
SEED_NULL = 42

# Export PDBs for the top-K most closed messages
EXPORT_TOP_K_PDB = 10
EXPORT_DIR = "pdb_exports"

# Define closure significance threshold
P_CLOSURE_THRESH = 0.01

# =========================
# END CONFIG
# =========================


# ----------------------------
# SHA-256 CONSTANTS & PRIMITIVES
# ----------------------------

MASK32 = 0xFFFFFFFF
K = [
    0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5, 0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1, 0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5,
    0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be, 0x550c7dc3, 0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174,
    0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786, 0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc, 0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa, 0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da,
    0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7, 0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147, 0x06ca6351, 0x14292967,
    0x27b70a85, 0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13, 0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85,
    0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b, 0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3, 0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070,
    0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5, 0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a, 0x5b9cca4f, 0x682e6ff3,
    0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208, 0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2
]

def rotr(x: int, n: int) -> int:
    return ((x >> n) | (x << (32 - n))) & MASK32

def Ch(x: int, y: int, z: int) -> int:
    return (x & y) ^ ((~x & MASK32) & z)

def Maj(x: int, y: int, z: int) -> int:
    return (x & y) ^ (x & z) ^ (y & z)

def Sigma0(x: int) -> int:
    return rotr(x, 2) ^ rotr(x, 13) ^ rotr(x, 22)

def Sigma1(x: int) -> int:
    return rotr(x, 6) ^ rotr(x, 11) ^ rotr(x, 25)

def sigma0(x: int) -> int:
    return rotr(x, 7) ^ rotr(x, 18) ^ (x >> 3)

def sigma1(x: int) -> int:
    return rotr(x, 17) ^ rotr(x, 19) ^ (x >> 10)


# ----------------------------
# SHA SINGLE-BLOCK T1 TRACE
# ----------------------------

def pad_message_single_block(message: bytes) -> bytes:
    """
    Standard SHA-256 padding for a message expected to fit in one 512-bit block after padding.
    Returns the first 64 bytes of the padded message.
    """
    msg_len_bits = len(message) * 8
    m = message + b'\x80'
    while (len(m) * 8) % 512 != 448:
        m += b'\x00'
    m += struct.pack('>Q', msg_len_bits)
    return m[:64]

def extract_T1_trace_single_block(message: bytes) -> List[int]:
    """
    Generates the 64-round T1 execution log for a *single* 512-bit block.
    """
    block = pad_message_single_block(message)
    W = list(struct.unpack('>16L', block))
    for i in range(16, 64):
        W.append((sigma1(W[i-2]) + W[i-7] + sigma0(W[i-15]) + W[i-16]) & MASK32)

    a, b, c, d, e, f, g, h = (
        0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a,
        0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab, 0x5be0cd19
    )

    T1_trace = []
    for t in range(64):
        T1 = (h + Sigma1(e) + Ch(e, f, g) + K[t] + W[t]) & MASK32
        T1_trace.append(T1)

        T2 = (Sigma0(a) + Maj(a, b, c)) & MASK32
        h, g, f, e = g, f, e, (d + T1) & MASK32
        d, c, b, a = c, b, a, (T1 + T2) & MASK32

    return T1_trace


# ----------------------------
# 1D TRACE -> 3D CHAIN (ISOTROPIC)
# ----------------------------

def map_T1_to_3D_isotropic(T1_trace: List[int], bond_length: float) -> List[Tuple[float, float, float]]:
    """
    Isotropic sphere sampling:
      u = low16/65535 in [0,1]
      v = high16/65535 in [0,1]
      theta = acos(1 - 2u)
      phi = 2pi v
    """
    coords = [(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)]
    x = y = z = 0.0

    for t in range(1, 64):
        T1 = T1_trace[t]
        low16  = T1 & 0xFFFF
        high16 = (T1 >> 16) & 0xFFFF

        u = low16 / 0xFFFF
        v = high16 / 0xFFFF

        theta = math.acos(1.0 - 2.0 * u)
        phi = (2.0 * math.pi) * v

        dx = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.cos(phi)
        dy = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.sin(phi)
        dz = bond_length * math.cos(theta)

        x += dx; y += dy; z += dz
        coords.append((x, y, z))

    return coords

def generate_random_walk_isotropic(N: int, bond_length: float) -> List[Tuple[float, float, float]]:
    coords = [(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)]
    x = y = z = 0.0
    for _ in range(1, N):
        u = random.random()
        v = random.random()
        theta = math.acos(1.0 - 2.0 * u)
        phi = (2.0 * math.pi) * v

        dx = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.cos(phi)
        dy = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.sin(phi)
        dz = bond_length * math.cos(theta)

        x += dx; y += dy; z += dz
        coords.append((x, y, z))
    return coords


# ----------------------------
# METRICS
# ----------------------------

def calculate_metrics(coords: List[Tuple[float, float, float]],
                      bond_length: float,
                      contact_cutoff: float,
                      min_seq_sep: int) -> Dict[str, float]:
    N = len(coords)
    if N < 2:
        return {"N": float(N), "L": 0.0, "Rg": 0.0, "Ree": 0.0, "r_rw": 0.0, "r_ee": 0.0, "contact_density": 0.0}

    cmx = sum(p[0] for p in coords) / N
    cmy = sum(p[1] for p in coords) / N
    cmz = sum(p[2] for p in coords) / N

    s = 0.0
    for (x, y, z) in coords:
        dx = x - cmx; dy = y - cmy; dz = z - cmz
        s += dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz
    Rg = math.sqrt(s / N)

    x0, y0, z0 = coords[0]
    x1, y1, z1 = coords[-1]
    Ree = math.sqrt((x1-x0)**2 + (y1-y0)**2 + (z1-z0)**2)

    L = (N - 1) * bond_length
    r_rw = Rg / (bond_length * math.sqrt(N))
    r_ee = (Rg / Ree) if Ree > 0 else float("inf")

    cut2 = contact_cutoff * contact_cutoff
    contacts = 0
    possible = 0
    for i in range(N):
        xi, yi, zi = coords[i]
        for j in range(i + min_seq_sep, N):
            xj, yj, zj = coords[j]
            dx = xi - xj; dy = yi - yj; dz = zi - zj
            d2 = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz
            possible += 1
            if d2 < cut2:
                contacts += 1
    contact_density = (contacts / possible) if possible else 0.0

    return {
        "N": float(N),
        "L": L,
        "Rg": Rg,
        "Ree": Ree,
        "r_rw": r_rw,
        "r_ee": r_ee,
        "contact_density": contact_density,
    }


# ----------------------------
# STATS HELPERS (empirical p-values)
# ----------------------------

def empirical_p_ge(x: float, xs: List[float]) -> float:
    n = len(xs)
    k = sum(1 for v in xs if v >= x)
    return (k + 1) / (n + 1)

def empirical_p_le(x: float, xs: List[float]) -> float:
    n = len(xs)
    k = sum(1 for v in xs if v <= x)
    return (k + 1) / (n + 1)

def mean(xs: List[float]) -> float:
    return sum(xs) / len(xs) if xs else 0.0

def sample_std(xs: List[float]) -> float:
    n = len(xs)
    if n <= 1:
        return 0.0
    mu = mean(xs)
    var = sum((x - mu) ** 2 for x in xs) / (n - 1)
    return math.sqrt(var)

def percentile_rank_le(x: float, xs: List[float]) -> float:
    """Empirical percentile in [0,1]: fraction of samples <= x."""
    n = len(xs)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    return sum(1 for v in xs if v <= x) / n


# ----------------------------
# PDB EXPORT
# ----------------------------

def export_to_pdb(coords: List[Tuple[float, float, float]], filename: str) -> None:
    with open(filename, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("HEADER    SHA-256 HASH TOPOLOGICAL MATRIX\n")
        f.write("REMARK    Generated via T1 Trace Execution Log\n")
        for i, (x, y, z) in enumerate(coords, start=1):
            f.write(f"ATOM  {i:5d}  CA  HSH A{i:4d}    {x:8.3f}{y:8.3f}{z:8.3f}  1.00  0.00           C\n")
        for i in range(1, len(coords)):
            f.write(f"CONECT{i:5d}{i+1:5d}\n")
        f.write("END\n")


# ----------------------------
# MESSAGE GENERATION
# ----------------------------

WORDS = [
    # small built-in list (no external files). Expand if you want.
    "GlassKey", "Nexus", "fold", "hash", "protein", "Intel", "carbon",
    "scope", "drift", "triplex", "pi", "phi", "e", "Sarrus",
    "collapse", "residue", "operator", "gap", "bandwidth", "observer"
]

def generate_messages(mode: str, n: int) -> List[bytes]:
    msgs = []
    if mode == "random_bytes":
        for _ in range(n):
            msgs.append(bytes(random.getrandbits(8) for _ in range(RANDOM_MSG_LEN)))
    elif mode == "words":
        for _ in range(n):
            w = random.choice(WORDS)
            msgs.append(w.encode("utf-8"))
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown MODE={mode!r}")
    return msgs


# ----------------------------
# PER-MESSAGE ANALYSIS
# ----------------------------

def analyze_message(msg: bytes) -> Dict[str, float]:
    """
    Compute SHA metrics + null-model p-values for this message.
    Returns a record dict.
    """
    # SHA chain
    T1 = extract_T1_trace_single_block(msg)
    sha_coords = map_T1_to_3D_isotropic(T1, bond_length=BOND_LENGTH)
    sha = calculate_metrics(sha_coords, BOND_LENGTH, CONTACT_CUTOFF, MIN_SEQ_SEP)
    N = int(sha["N"])

    # Null model
    # Use a deterministic per-message seed to make repeated runs stable while still varying per message.
    # (So changing batch order doesn't change per-message null baselines.)
    seed_per_msg = (SEED_NULL * 1_000_000) ^ int.from_bytes(msg[:4].ljust(4, b"\x00"), "big")
    random.seed(seed_per_msg)

    rw_Ree = []
    rw_r_ee = []
    rw_r_rw = []
    rw_cd = []

    for _ in range(RW_SAMPLES):
        rw_coords = generate_random_walk_isotropic(N, BOND_LENGTH)
        rw = calculate_metrics(rw_coords, BOND_LENGTH, CONTACT_CUTOFF, MIN_SEQ_SEP)
        rw_Ree.append(rw["Ree"])
        rw_r_ee.append(rw["r_ee"])
        rw_r_rw.append(rw["r_rw"])
        rw_cd.append(rw["contact_density"])

    # Empirical p-values:
    # - closure: Ree is small => left tail
    p_Ree = empirical_p_le(sha["Ree"], rw_Ree)
    # - r_ee is large => right tail
    p_r_ee = empirical_p_ge(sha["r_ee"], rw_r_ee)

    # Also record percentiles (useful for summaries/plots later)
    pct_Ree = percentile_rank_le(sha["Ree"], rw_Ree)  # same as without smoothing

    rec = {
        "msg_hex": msg.hex(),
        "msg_utf8": msg.decode("utf-8", errors="replace"),
        "N": sha["N"],
        "Rg": sha["Rg"],
        "Ree": sha["Ree"],
        "r_rw": sha["r_rw"],
        "r_ee": sha["r_ee"],
        "contact_density": sha["contact_density"],
        "p_Ree": p_Ree,
        "p_r_ee": p_r_ee,
        "pct_Ree": pct_Ree,
        # baseline stats (optional, but handy)
        "rw_Ree_mu": mean(rw_Ree),
        "rw_Ree_sd": sample_std(rw_Ree),
        "rw_r_ee_mu": mean(rw_r_ee),
        "rw_r_ee_sd": sample_std(rw_r_ee),
    }

    # For exporting later, return coords too (but only if you want—keeps memory light otherwise)
    rec["_coords"] = sha_coords
    return rec


# ----------------------------
# MAIN BATCH RUN
# ----------------------------

def main():
    random.seed(SEED_MESSAGES)
    msgs = generate_messages(MODE, BATCH_MESSAGES)

    print("=" * 78)
    print("BATCH RUN: SHA-256 Topological Closure vs RW Null")
    print("=" * 78)
    print(f"MODE: {MODE}")
    print(f"BATCH_MESSAGES: {BATCH_MESSAGES}")
    if MODE == "random_bytes":
        print(f"RANDOM_MSG_LEN: {RANDOM_MSG_LEN}")
    print(f"RW_SAMPLES per message: {RW_SAMPLES}")
    print(f"BOND_LENGTH: {BOND_LENGTH} Å | CONTACT_CUTOFF: {CONTACT_CUTOFF} Å | MIN_SEQ_SEP: {MIN_SEQ_SEP}")
    print(f"Closure threshold: p(Ree) <= {P_CLOSURE_THRESH}")
    print("=" * 78)
    print()

    records = []
    for idx, msg in enumerate(msgs, start=1):
        rec = analyze_message(msg)
        records.append(rec)

        # Progress print (no truncation)
        print(f"[{idx:4d}/{BATCH_MESSAGES}] msg_utf8={rec['msg_utf8']!r}  Ree={rec['Ree']:.4f}  p_Ree={rec['p_Ree']:.6f}  r_ee={rec['r_ee']:.4f}  p_r_ee={rec['p_r_ee']:.6f}")

    # Summaries
    n = len(records)
    closed = [r for r in records if r["p_Ree"] <= P_CLOSURE_THRESH]
    frac_closed = len(closed) / n if n else 0.0

    pvals = [r["p_Ree"] for r in records]
    pvals_sorted = sorted(pvals)

    def quantile(xs: List[float], q: float) -> float:
        if not xs:
            return 0.0
        i = int(round(q * (len(xs) - 1)))
        return xs[max(0, min(len(xs)-1, i))]

    q01 = quantile(pvals_sorted, 0.01)
    q05 = quantile(pvals_sorted, 0.05)
    q50 = quantile(pvals_sorted, 0.50)
    q95 = quantile(pvals_sorted, 0.95)

    print("\n" + "=" * 78)
    print("SUMMARY (Closure via Ree vs RW null)")
    print("=" * 78)
    print(f"Messages tested: {n}")
    print(f"Count with p(Ree) <= {P_CLOSURE_THRESH}: {len(closed)}")
    print(f"Fraction with p(Ree) <= {P_CLOSURE_THRESH}: {frac_closed:.4f}")
    print("p(Ree) quantiles:")
    print(f"  q01={q01:.6f}  q05={q05:.6f}  q50={q50:.6f}  q95={q95:.6f}")
    print("=" * 78)

    # Top-K most closed (smallest p_Ree; tie-breaker smallest Ree)
    top = sorted(records, key=lambda r: (r["p_Ree"], r["Ree"]))[:EXPORT_TOP_K_PDB]

    print("\nTOP MOST-CLOSED MESSAGES (smallest p(Ree)) — FULL LIST")
    print("-" * 78)
    for rank, r in enumerate(top, start=1):
        print(
            f"[{rank:02d}] p_Ree={r['p_Ree']:.6f}  Ree={r['Ree']:.4f}  "
            f"Rg={r['Rg']:.4f}  r_ee={r['r_ee']:.4f}  "
            f"rw_Ree={r['rw_Ree_mu']:.4f}±{r['rw_Ree_sd']:.4f}  "
            f"msg_utf8={r['msg_utf8']!r}  msg_hex={r['msg_hex']}"
        )

    # Export PDBs
    if EXPORT_TOP_K_PDB > 0:
        os.makedirs(EXPORT_DIR, exist_ok=True)
        print("\nExporting PDBs for top-K most-closed messages...")
        for rank, r in enumerate(top, start=1):
            fn = os.path.join(EXPORT_DIR, f"rank_{rank:02d}_p_{r['p_Ree']:.6f}_Ree_{r['Ree']:.3f}.pdb")
            export_to_pdb(r["_coords"], fn)
        print(f"Export complete: {EXPORT_DIR}/ (contains {len(top)} .pdb files)")

    print("\nDONE.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# chatgpt response

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
SHA-256 TOPOLOGICAL MAPPER — Batch Experiment (Next Run, Shared Null Model)

Key upgrade vs prior run
------------------------
Instead of generating RW_SAMPLES *per message*, we precompute ONE large
isotropic Random Walk null pool for N=64 once, then score every message
against the same baseline. This makes per-message p-values comparable,
reduces noise, and is much faster.

No external dependencies. Prints full lists (no truncation).
Single 512-bit block only (64 rounds) by design.

"""

import math
import struct
import random
import os
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict

# =========================
# CONFIG (EDIT THESE)
# =========================

BOND_LENGTH = 3.8
CONTACT_CUTOFF = 8.0
MIN_SEQ_SEP = 4

# Batch size: how many messages to test
BATCH_MESSAGES = 200

# Message generation mode: "random_bytes" or "words"
MODE = "random_bytes"

# For MODE="random_bytes"
RANDOM_MSG_LEN = 8

# Shared null model pool size (bigger = tighter p-values; resolution ~ 1/(RW_POOL+1))
RW_POOL = 100_000

# Reproducibility
SEED_MESSAGES = 123
SEED_NULL = 42

# Export PDBs for the top-K most closed messages (by p(Ree), then Ree)
EXPORT_TOP_K_PDB = 10
EXPORT_DIR = "pdb_exports"

# Define closure significance threshold
P_CLOSURE_THRESH = 0.01

# Also track "tight closure" (stricter tail)
P_CLOSURE_TIGHT = 0.001

# =========================
# END CONFIG
# =========================


# ----------------------------
# SHA-256 CONSTANTS & PRIMITIVES
# ----------------------------

MASK32 = 0xFFFFFFFF
K = [
    0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5, 0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1, 0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5,
    0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be, 0x550c7dc3, 0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174,
    0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786, 0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc, 0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa, 0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da,
    0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7, 0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147, 0x06ca6351, 0x14292967,
    0x27b70a85, 0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13, 0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85,
    0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b, 0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3, 0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070,
    0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5, 0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a, 0x5b9cca4f, 0x682e6ff3,
    0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208, 0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2
]

def rotr(x: int, n: int) -> int:
    return ((x >> n) | (x << (32 - n))) & MASK32

def Ch(x: int, y: int, z: int) -> int:
    return (x & y) ^ ((~x & MASK32) & z)

def Maj(x: int, y: int, z: int) -> int:
    return (x & y) ^ (x & z) ^ (y & z)

def Sigma0(x: int) -> int:
    return rotr(x, 2) ^ rotr(x, 13) ^ rotr(x, 22)

def Sigma1(x: int) -> int:
    return rotr(x, 6) ^ rotr(x, 11) ^ rotr(x, 25)

def sigma0(x: int) -> int:
    return rotr(x, 7) ^ rotr(x, 18) ^ (x >> 3)

def sigma1(x: int) -> int:
    return rotr(x, 17) ^ rotr(x, 19) ^ (x >> 10)


# ----------------------------
# SHA SINGLE-BLOCK T1 TRACE
# ----------------------------

def pad_message_single_block(message: bytes) -> bytes:
    """
    Standard SHA-256 padding for a message expected to fit in one 512-bit block after padding.
    Returns the first 64 bytes of the padded message.
    """
    msg_len_bits = len(message) * 8
    m = message + b'\x80'
    while (len(m) * 8) % 512 != 448:
        m += b'\x00'
    m += struct.pack('>Q', msg_len_bits)
    return m[:64]

def extract_T1_trace_single_block(message: bytes) -> List[int]:
    """
    Generates the 64-round T1 execution log for a *single* 512-bit block.
    """
    block = pad_message_single_block(message)
    W = list(struct.unpack('>16L', block))
    for i in range(16, 64):
        W.append((sigma1(W[i-2]) + W[i-7] + sigma0(W[i-15]) + W[i-16]) & MASK32)

    a, b, c, d, e, f, g, h = (
        0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a,
        0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab, 0x5be0cd19
    )

    T1_trace = []
    for t in range(64):
        T1 = (h + Sigma1(e) + Ch(e, f, g) + K[t] + W[t]) & MASK32
        T1_trace.append(T1)

        T2 = (Sigma0(a) + Maj(a, b, c)) & MASK32
        h, g, f, e = g, f, e, (d + T1) & MASK32
        d, c, b, a = c, b, a, (T1 + T2) & MASK32

    return T1_trace


# ----------------------------
# 1D TRACE -> 3D CHAIN (ISOTROPIC)
# ----------------------------

def map_T1_to_3D_isotropic(T1_trace: List[int], bond_length: float) -> List[Tuple[float, float, float]]:
    """
    Isotropic sphere sampling:
      u = low16/65535 in [0,1]
      v = high16/65535 in [0,1]
      theta = acos(1 - 2u)
      phi = 2pi v
    """
    coords = [(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)]
    x = y = z = 0.0

    for t in range(1, 64):
        T1 = T1_trace[t]
        low16  = T1 & 0xFFFF
        high16 = (T1 >> 16) & 0xFFFF

        u = low16 / 0xFFFF
        v = high16 / 0xFFFF

        theta = math.acos(1.0 - 2.0 * u)
        phi = (2.0 * math.pi) * v

        dx = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.cos(phi)
        dy = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.sin(phi)
        dz = bond_length * math.cos(theta)

        x += dx; y += dy; z += dz
        coords.append((x, y, z))

    return coords

def generate_random_walk_isotropic(N: int, bond_length: float) -> List[Tuple[float, float, float]]:
    coords = [(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)]
    x = y = z = 0.0
    for _ in range(1, N):
        u = random.random()
        v = random.random()
        theta = math.acos(1.0 - 2.0 * u)
        phi = (2.0 * math.pi) * v

        dx = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.cos(phi)
        dy = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.sin(phi)
        dz = bond_length * math.cos(theta)

        x += dx; y += dy; z += dz
        coords.append((x, y, z))
    return coords


# ----------------------------
# METRICS
# ----------------------------

def calculate_metrics(coords: List[Tuple[float, float, float]],
                      bond_length: float,
                      contact_cutoff: float,
                      min_seq_sep: int) -> Dict[str, float]:
    N = len(coords)
    if N < 2:
        return {"N": float(N), "L": 0.0, "Rg": 0.0, "Ree": 0.0, "r_rw": 0.0, "r_ee": 0.0, "contact_density": 0.0}

    cmx = sum(p[0] for p in coords) / N
    cmy = sum(p[1] for p in coords) / N
    cmz = sum(p[2] for p in coords) / N

    s = 0.0
    for (x, y, z) in coords:
        dx = x - cmx; dy = y - cmy; dz = z - cmz
        s += dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz
    Rg = math.sqrt(s / N)

    x0, y0, z0 = coords[0]
    x1, y1, z1 = coords[-1]
    Ree = math.sqrt((x1-x0)**2 + (y1-y0)**2 + (z1-z0)**2)

    L = (N - 1) * bond_length
    r_rw = Rg / (bond_length * math.sqrt(N))
    r_ee = (Rg / Ree) if Ree > 0 else float("inf")

    cut2 = contact_cutoff * contact_cutoff
    contacts = 0
    possible = 0
    for i in range(N):
        xi, yi, zi = coords[i]
        for j in range(i + min_seq_sep, N):
            xj, yj, zj = coords[j]
            dx = xi - xj; dy = yi - yj; dz = zi - zj
            d2 = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz
            possible += 1
            if d2 < cut2:
                contacts += 1
    contact_density = (contacts / possible) if possible else 0.0

    return {
        "N": float(N),
        "L": L,
        "Rg": Rg,
        "Ree": Ree,
        "r_rw": r_rw,
        "r_ee": r_ee,
        "contact_density": contact_density,
    }


# ----------------------------
# STATS HELPERS
# ----------------------------

def empirical_p_ge(x: float, xs: List[float]) -> float:
    n = len(xs)
    k = 0
    for v in xs:
        if v >= x:
            k += 1
    return (k + 1) / (n + 1)

def empirical_p_le(x: float, xs: List[float]) -> float:
    n = len(xs)
    k = 0
    for v in xs:
        if v <= x:
            k += 1
    return (k + 1) / (n + 1)

def mean(xs: List[float]) -> float:
    return sum(xs) / len(xs) if xs else 0.0

def sample_std(xs: List[float]) -> float:
    n = len(xs)
    if n <= 1:
        return 0.0
    mu = mean(xs)
    var = 0.0
    for x in xs:
        var += (x - mu) ** 2
    var /= (n - 1)
    return math.sqrt(var)

def quantile_sorted(xs_sorted: List[float], q: float) -> float:
    if not xs_sorted:
        return 0.0
    if q <= 0:
        return xs_sorted[0]
    if q >= 1:
        return xs_sorted[-1]
    i = int(round(q * (len(xs_sorted) - 1)))
    if i < 0:
        i = 0
    if i >= len(xs_sorted):
        i = len(xs_sorted) - 1
    return xs_sorted[i]

def ks_statistic(x: List[float], y: List[float]) -> float:
    """
    Two-sample KS statistic D = sup |F_x(t) - F_y(t)|
    No scipy. O((n+m) log (n+m))
    """
    xs = sorted(x)
    ys = sorted(y)
    n = len(xs)
    m = len(ys)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return 0.0

    i = j = 0
    Fx = Fy = 0.0
    D = 0.0

    while i < n or j < m:
        if j >= m or (i < n and xs[i] <= ys[j]):
            t = xs[i]
        else:
            t = ys[j]

        while i < n and xs[i] <= t:
            i += 1
        while j < m and ys[j] <= t:
            j += 1

        Fx = i / n
        Fy = j / m
        d = abs(Fx - Fy)
        if d > D:
            D = d

    return D

def ks_pvalue_approx(D: float, n: int, m: int) -> float:
    """
    Asymptotic KS p-value approximation (good enough for a sanity check).
    """
    if n <= 0 or m <= 0:
        return 1.0
    en = math.sqrt((n * m) / (n + m))
    lam = (en + 0.12 + 0.11 / en) * D
    # Q_KS(lam) ≈ 2 * sum_{k=1..∞} (-1)^{k-1} exp(-2 k^2 lam^2)
    # truncate when term small
    s = 0.0
    k = 1
    while True:
        term = 2.0 * ((-1) ** (k - 1)) * math.exp(-2.0 * (k * k) * (lam * lam))
        s += term
        if abs(term) < 1e-10:
            break
        k += 1
        if k > 10_000:
            break
    # clamp
    if s < 0.0:
        s = 0.0
    if s > 1.0:
        s = 1.0
    return s


# ----------------------------
# PDB EXPORT
# ----------------------------

def export_to_pdb(coords: List[Tuple[float, float, float]], filename: str) -> None:
    with open(filename, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("HEADER    SHA-256 HASH TOPOLOGICAL MATRIX\n")
        f.write("REMARK    Generated via T1 Trace Execution Log\n")
        for i, (x, y, z) in enumerate(coords, start=1):
            f.write(f"ATOM  {i:5d}  CA  HSH A{i:4d}    {x:8.3f}{y:8.3f}{z:8.3f}  1.00  0.00           C\n")
        for i in range(1, len(coords)):
            f.write(f"CONECT{i:5d}{i+1:5d}\n")
        f.write("END\n")


# ----------------------------
# MESSAGE GENERATION
# ----------------------------

WORDS = [
    "GlassKey", "Nexus", "fold", "hash", "protein", "Intel", "carbon",
    "scope", "drift", "triplex", "pi", "phi", "e", "Sarrus",
    "collapse", "residue", "operator", "gap", "bandwidth", "observer"
]

def generate_messages(mode: str, n: int) -> List[bytes]:
    msgs = []
    if mode == "random_bytes":
        for _ in range(n):
            msgs.append(bytes(random.getrandbits(8) for _ in range(RANDOM_MSG_LEN)))
    elif mode == "words":
        for _ in range(n):
            w = random.choice(WORDS)
            msgs.append(w.encode("utf-8"))
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown MODE={mode!r}")
    return msgs


# ----------------------------
# SHARED NULL MODEL
# ----------------------------

def build_shared_null_pool(N: int, pool_size: int) -> Dict[str, List[float]]:
    """
    Build one large RW null pool (same N, same metric definitions).
    """
    rw_Ree = []
    rw_r_ee = []
    rw_r_rw = []
    rw_cd = []

    for _ in range(pool_size):
        rw_coords = generate_random_walk_isotropic(N, BOND_LENGTH)
        rw = calculate_metrics(rw_coords, BOND_LENGTH, CONTACT_CUTOFF, MIN_SEQ_SEP)
        rw_Ree.append(rw["Ree"])
        rw_r_ee.append(rw["r_ee"])
        rw_r_rw.append(rw["r_rw"])
        rw_cd.append(rw["contact_density"])

    return {
        "Ree": rw_Ree,
        "r_ee": rw_r_ee,
        "r_rw": rw_r_rw,
        "cd": rw_cd,
    }


# ----------------------------
# PER-MESSAGE ANALYSIS (vs shared pool)
# ----------------------------

def analyze_message(msg: bytes, null_pool: Dict[str, List[float]]) -> Dict[str, object]:
    """
    Compute SHA metrics + null-model p-values for this message.
    Returns a record dict (includes coords for export).
    """
    T1 = extract_T1_trace_single_block(msg)
    sha_coords = map_T1_to_3D_isotropic(T1, bond_length=BOND_LENGTH)
    sha = calculate_metrics(sha_coords, BOND_LENGTH, CONTACT_CUTOFF, MIN_SEQ_SEP)

    # Empirical p-values:
    # - closure: Ree small => left tail
    p_Ree = empirical_p_le(sha["Ree"], null_pool["Ree"])
    # - r_ee large => right tail
    p_r_ee = empirical_p_ge(sha["r_ee"], null_pool["r_ee"])
    # - contact density small/large can also be checked; keep both tails if you want later

    rec = {
        "msg_hex": msg.hex(),
        "msg_utf8": msg.decode("utf-8", errors="replace"),
        "N": int(sha["N"]),
        "L": sha["L"],
        "Rg": sha["Rg"],
        "Ree": sha["Ree"],
        "r_rw": sha["r_rw"],
        "r_ee": sha["r_ee"],
        "contact_density": sha["contact_density"],
        "p_Ree": p_Ree,
        "p_r_ee": p_r_ee,
        "_coords": sha_coords,
    }
    return rec


# ----------------------------
# MAIN
# ----------------------------

def main():
    N = 64  # fixed by design

    print("=" * 78)
    print("BATCH RUN: SHA-256 Topological Closure vs SHARED RW Null")
    print("=" * 78)
    print(f"MODE: {MODE}")
    print(f"BATCH_MESSAGES: {BATCH_MESSAGES}")
    if MODE == "random_bytes":
        print(f"RANDOM_MSG_LEN: {RANDOM_MSG_LEN}")
    print(f"RW_POOL (shared null size): {RW_POOL}")
    print(f"BOND_LENGTH: {BOND_LENGTH} Å | CONTACT_CUTOFF: {CONTACT_CUTOFF} Å | MIN_SEQ_SEP: {MIN_SEQ_SEP}")
    print(f"Closure threshold: p(Ree) <= {P_CLOSURE_THRESH} (tight={P_CLOSURE_TIGHT})")
    print(f"Empirical p-value resolution: ~1/{RW_POOL+1}")
    print("=" * 78)
    print()

    # Build shared null pool
    random.seed(SEED_NULL)
    print(f"Building shared RW null pool: N={N}, pool={RW_POOL} ...")
    null_pool = build_shared_null_pool(N, RW_POOL)
    mu_Ree = mean(null_pool["Ree"]); sd_Ree = sample_std(null_pool["Ree"])
    mu_r_ee = mean(null_pool["r_ee"]); sd_r_ee = sample_std(null_pool["r_ee"])
    mu_r_rw = mean(null_pool["r_rw"]); sd_r_rw = sample_std(null_pool["r_rw"])
    mu_cd = mean(null_pool["cd"]); sd_cd = sample_std(null_pool["cd"])
    print("Null pool stats:")
    print(f"  Ree:   {mu_Ree:.4f} ± {sd_Ree:.4f}")
    print(f"  r_ee:  {mu_r_ee:.4f} ± {sd_r_ee:.4f}")
    print(f"  r_rw:  {mu_r_rw:.4f} ± {sd_r_rw:.4f}")
    print(f"  cd:    {mu_cd:.4f} ± {sd_cd:.4f}")
    print()

    # Generate messages
    random.seed(SEED_MESSAGES)
    msgs = generate_messages(MODE, BATCH_MESSAGES)

    # Analyze
    records = []
    for idx, msg in enumerate(msgs, start=1):
        rec = analyze_message(msg, null_pool)
        records.append(rec)

        # progress (no truncation)
        print(
            f"[{idx:4d}/{BATCH_MESSAGES}] msg_utf8={rec['msg_utf8']!r}  "
            f"Ree={rec['Ree']:.4f}  p_Ree={rec['p_Ree']:.6f}  "
            f"r_ee={rec['r_ee']:.4f}  p_r_ee={rec['p_r_ee']:.6f}"
        )

    # Summaries
    n = len(records)
    pvals_Ree = [r["p_Ree"] for r in records]
    pvals_Ree_sorted = sorted(pvals_Ree)

    closed_01 = [r for r in records if r["p_Ree"] <= P_CLOSURE_THRESH]
    closed_001 = [r for r in records if r["p_Ree"] <= P_CLOSURE_TIGHT]

    print("\n" + "=" * 78)
    print("SUMMARY (Closure via Ree vs SHARED RW null)")
    print("=" * 78)
    print(f"Messages tested: {n}")
    print(f"Count with p(Ree) <= {P_CLOSURE_THRESH}: {len(closed_01)}")
    print(f"Fraction with p(Ree) <= {P_CLOSURE_THRESH}: {len(closed_01)/n if n else 0.0:.6f}")
    print(f"Count with p(Ree) <= {P_CLOSURE_TIGHT}: {len(closed_001)}")
    print(f"Fraction with p(Ree) <= {P_CLOSURE_TIGHT}: {len(closed_001)/n if n else 0.0:.6f}")
    print("p(Ree) quantiles:")
    print(
        f"  q01={quantile_sorted(pvals_Ree_sorted, 0.01):.6f}  "
        f"q05={quantile_sorted(pvals_Ree_sorted, 0.05):.6f}  "
        f"q50={quantile_sorted(pvals_Ree_sorted, 0.50):.6f}  "
        f"q95={quantile_sorted(pvals_Ree_sorted, 0.95):.6f}"
    )
    print("=" * 78)

    # Distribution-level check (KS) for Ree and r_ee vs shared null
    sha_Ree = [r["Ree"] for r in records]
    sha_r_ee = [r["r_ee"] for r in records]
    D_Ree = ks_statistic(sha_Ree, null_pool["Ree"])
    pks_Ree = ks_pvalue_approx(D_Ree, len(sha_Ree), len(null_pool["Ree"]))
    D_r_ee = ks_statistic(sha_r_ee, null_pool["r_ee"])
    pks_r_ee = ks_pvalue_approx(D_r_ee, len(sha_r_ee), len(null_pool["r_ee"]))

    print("\nKS sanity checks (batch distribution vs shared null):")
    print(f"  KS(Ree):  D={D_Ree:.6f}  approx_p={pks_Ree:.6g}")
    print(f"  KS(r_ee): D={D_r_ee:.6f}  approx_p={pks_r_ee:.6g}")

    # Top-K most closed (smallest p_Ree; tie-breaker smallest Ree)
    top = sorted(records, key=lambda r: (r["p_Ree"], r["Ree"]))[:EXPORT_TOP_K_PDB]

    print("\nTOP MOST-CLOSED MESSAGES (smallest p(Ree)) — FULL LIST")
    print("-" * 78)
    for rank, r in enumerate(top, start=1):
        # z-scores relative to shared null (using sample sd)
        z_Ree = (r["Ree"] - mu_Ree) / sd_Ree if sd_Ree > 0 else 0.0
        z_r_ee = (r["r_ee"] - mu_r_ee) / sd_r_ee if sd_r_ee > 0 else 0.0
        z_cd = (r["contact_density"] - mu_cd) / sd_cd if sd_cd > 0 else 0.0
        z_r_rw = (r["r_rw"] - mu_r_rw) / sd_r_rw if sd_r_rw > 0 else 0.0

        print(
            f"[{rank:02d}] p_Ree={r['p_Ree']:.6f}  Ree={r['Ree']:.4f}  z_Ree={z_Ree:+.3f}  "
            f"Rg={r['Rg']:.4f}  r_ee={r['r_ee']:.4f}  z_r_ee={z_r_ee:+.3f}  p_r_ee={r['p_r_ee']:.6f}  "
            f"r_rw={r['r_rw']:.4f}  z_r_rw={z_r_rw:+.3f}  "
            f"cd={r['contact_density']:.4f}  z_cd={z_cd:+.3f}  "
            f"null_Ree={mu_Ree:.4f}±{sd_Ree:.4f}  "
            f"msg_utf8={r['msg_utf8']!r}  msg_hex={r['msg_hex']}"
        )

    # Export PDBs
    if EXPORT_TOP_K_PDB > 0:
        os.makedirs(EXPORT_DIR, exist_ok=True)
        print("\nExporting PDBs for top-K most-closed messages...")
        for rank, r in enumerate(top, start=1):
            fn = os.path.join(EXPORT_DIR, f"rank_{rank:02d}_p_{r['p_Ree']:.6f}_Ree_{r['Ree']:.3f}.pdb")
            export_to_pdb(r["_coords"], fn)
        print(f"Export complete: {EXPORT_DIR}/ (contains {len(top)} .pdb files)")

    print("\nDONE.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

# chatgpt response

Your run is behaving like a null-consistent instrument.

**What you just measured (interpreting the printout):**

- Under a true null, with threshold $\alpha=0.01$ and $n=200$ messages, the expected count is  
  $$
  \mathbb{E}[C]=n\alpha = 200\cdot 0.01 = 2
  $$
  Observing **1** “closed” message is not surprising.

- Your KS checks are also **not** screaming “global shift”:
  - $D_{\text{KS}}(\text{Ree})\approx 0.0756,\; p\approx 0.196$ → consistent with null.
  - $D_{\text{KS}}(r_{ee})\approx 0.0923,\; p\approx 0.0625$ → mild tension, not decisive.

- The “winner” message is extreme on **both**:
  - $p(\text{Ree})=0.00546$
  - $p(r_{ee})=0.00258$
  But with multiple testing, extremes will appear. The correct next step is to **test enrichment of joint-extremes**, not celebrate a single hit.

So the **next run** should do two things:

1) **Scale up $n$** (more messages) so we can see whether the tail count is stable or just noise.  
2) **Use a proper multiple-testing / joint-tail statistic**, e.g. Fisher’s method on $(p_{\text{Ree}}, p_{r_{ee}})$, and then run BH-FDR.

Below is the **full next-run code**: it keeps your shared RW null pool, adds:

- Batch size bump option (default 2000; change as needed)
- Fisher combined p-value:
  $$
  X = -2(\ln p_1 + \ln p_2),\quad X \sim \chi^2_{4}
  $$
- BH-FDR $q$-values across all messages
- A **sanity-control mode**: replace SHA T1 with random 32-bit values (pipeline control). If the SHA and CONTROL behave the same, you’ve got no signal (good to know).

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
SHA-256 TOPOLOGICAL MAPPER — Batch Experiment (Next Run, Joint-Tail + FDR)

Adds:
  - Larger batch support
  - Fisher combined p-value for (p_Ree, p_r_ee)
  - Benjamini–Hochberg FDR q-values
  - Optional CONTROL mode: random T1 traces (pipeline sanity check)

Single 512-bit block only (64 rounds) by design.
No external dependencies.
"""

import math
import struct
import random
import os
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict

# =========================
# CONFIG (EDIT THESE)
# =========================

BOND_LENGTH = 3.8
CONTACT_CUTOFF = 8.0
MIN_SEQ_SEP = 4

# Bigger n for stability
BATCH_MESSAGES = 2000

MODE = "random_bytes"          # "random_bytes" or "words"
RANDOM_MSG_LEN = 8

# Shared null pool size (resolution ~ 1/(RW_POOL+1))
RW_POOL = 100000

SEED_MESSAGES = 123
SEED_NULL = 42

EXPORT_TOP_K_PDB = 25
EXPORT_DIR = "pdb_exports"

# Primary thresholds (raw p)
P_CLOSURE_THRESH = 0.01        # for Ree alone
P_JOINT_THRESH = 0.01          # for Fisher combined p
FDR_Q_THRESH = 0.10            # q-value threshold for discoveries

# CONTROL: if True, ignore SHA and use random 32-bit T1 per message (pipeline check)
CONTROL_RANDOM_T1 = False
CONTROL_SEED = 999

# =========================
# END CONFIG
# =========================


# ----------------------------
# SHA-256 CONSTANTS & PRIMITIVES
# ----------------------------

MASK32 = 0xFFFFFFFF
K = [
    0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5, 0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1, 0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5,
    0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be, 0x550c7dc3, 0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174,
    0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786, 0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc, 0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa, 0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da,
    0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7, 0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147, 0x06ca6351, 0x14292967,
    0x27b70a85, 0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13, 0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85,
    0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b, 0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3, 0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070,
    0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5, 0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a, 0x5b9cca4f, 0x682e6ff3,
    0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208, 0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2
]

def rotr(x: int, n: int) -> int:
    return ((x >> n) | (x << (32 - n))) & MASK32

def Ch(x: int, y: int, z: int) -> int:
    return (x & y) ^ ((~x & MASK32) & z)

def Maj(x: int, y: int, z: int) -> int:
    return (x & y) ^ (x & z) ^ (y & z)

def Sigma0(x: int) -> int:
    return rotr(x, 2) ^ rotr(x, 13) ^ rotr(x, 22)

def Sigma1(x: int) -> int:
    return rotr(x, 6) ^ rotr(x, 11) ^ rotr(x, 25)

def sigma0(x: int) -> int:
    return rotr(x, 7) ^ rotr(x, 18) ^ (x >> 3)

def sigma1(x: int) -> int:
    return rotr(x, 17) ^ rotr(x, 19) ^ (x >> 10)


# ----------------------------
# SHA SINGLE-BLOCK T1 TRACE
# ----------------------------

def pad_message_single_block(message: bytes) -> bytes:
    msg_len_bits = len(message) * 8
    m = message + b'\x80'
    while (len(m) * 8) % 512 != 448:
        m += b'\x00'
    m += struct.pack('>Q', msg_len_bits)
    return m[:64]

def extract_T1_trace_single_block(message: bytes) -> List[int]:
    block = pad_message_single_block(message)
    W = list(struct.unpack('>16L', block))
    for i in range(16, 64):
        W.append((sigma1(W[i-2]) + W[i-7] + sigma0(W[i-15]) + W[i-16]) & MASK32)

    a, b, c, d, e, f, g, h = (
        0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a,
        0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab, 0x5be0cd19
    )

    T1_trace = []
    for t in range(64):
        T1 = (h + Sigma1(e) + Ch(e, f, g) + K[t] + W[t]) & MASK32
        T1_trace.append(T1)

        T2 = (Sigma0(a) + Maj(a, b, c)) & MASK32
        h, g, f, e = g, f, e, (d + T1) & MASK32
        d, c, b, a = c, b, a, (T1 + T2) & MASK32

    return T1_trace

def control_random_T1_trace() -> List[int]:
    # 64 random 32-bit values (CONTROL)
    return [random.getrandbits(32) for _ in range(64)]


# ----------------------------
# 1D TRACE -> 3D CHAIN (ISOTROPIC)
# ----------------------------

def map_T1_to_3D_isotropic(T1_trace: List[int], bond_length: float) -> List[Tuple[float, float, float]]:
    coords = [(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)]
    x = y = z = 0.0

    for t in range(1, 64):
        T1 = T1_trace[t]
        low16  = T1 & 0xFFFF
        high16 = (T1 >> 16) & 0xFFFF

        u = low16 / 0xFFFF
        v = high16 / 0xFFFF

        theta = math.acos(1.0 - 2.0 * u)
        phi = (2.0 * math.pi) * v

        dx = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.cos(phi)
        dy = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.sin(phi)
        dz = bond_length * math.cos(theta)

        x += dx; y += dy; z += dz
        coords.append((x, y, z))

    return coords

def generate_random_walk_isotropic(N: int, bond_length: float) -> List[Tuple[float, float, float]]:
    coords = [(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)]
    x = y = z = 0.0
    for _ in range(1, N):
        u = random.random()
        v = random.random()
        theta = math.acos(1.0 - 2.0 * u)
        phi = (2.0 * math.pi) * v

        dx = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.cos(phi)
        dy = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.sin(phi)
        dz = bond_length * math.cos(theta)

        x += dx; y += dy; z += dz
        coords.append((x, y, z))
    return coords


# ----------------------------
# METRICS
# ----------------------------

def calculate_metrics(coords: List[Tuple[float, float, float]],
                      bond_length: float,
                      contact_cutoff: float,
                      min_seq_sep: int) -> Dict[str, float]:
    N = len(coords)
    cmx = sum(p[0] for p in coords) / N
    cmy = sum(p[1] for p in coords) / N
    cmz = sum(p[2] for p in coords) / N

    s = 0.0
    for (x, y, z) in coords:
        dx = x - cmx; dy = y - cmy; dz = z - cmz
        s += dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz
    Rg = math.sqrt(s / N)

    x0, y0, z0 = coords[0]
    x1, y1, z1 = coords[-1]
    Ree = math.sqrt((x1-x0)**2 + (y1-y0)**2 + (z1-z0)**2)

    L = (N - 1) * bond_length
    r_rw = Rg / (bond_length * math.sqrt(N))
    r_ee = (Rg / Ree) if Ree > 0 else float("inf")

    cut2 = contact_cutoff * contact_cutoff
    contacts = 0
    possible = 0
    for i in range(N):
        xi, yi, zi = coords[i]
        for j in range(i + min_seq_sep, N):
            xj, yj, zj = coords[j]
            dx = xi - xj; dy = yi - yj; dz = zi - zj
            d2 = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz
            possible += 1
            if d2 < cut2:
                contacts += 1
    contact_density = (contacts / possible) if possible else 0.0

    return {
        "N": float(N),
        "L": L,
        "Rg": Rg,
        "Ree": Ree,
        "r_rw": r_rw,
        "r_ee": r_ee,
        "contact_density": contact_density,
    }


# ----------------------------
# EMPIRICAL P + FISHER
# ----------------------------

def empirical_p_ge(x: float, xs: List[float]) -> float:
    n = len(xs)
    k = 0
    for v in xs:
        if v >= x:
            k += 1
    return (k + 1) / (n + 1)

def empirical_p_le(x: float, xs: List[float]) -> float:
    n = len(xs)
    k = 0
    for v in xs:
        if v <= x:
            k += 1
    return (k + 1) / (n + 1)

def fisher_chi2_sf_df4(x: float) -> float:
    """
    Survival function for chi-square with df=4:
      P(X >= x), X ~ Chi^2_4
    Closed form:
      SF = exp(-x/2) * (1 + x/2)
    """
    return math.exp(-x/2.0) * (1.0 + x/2.0)

def fisher_combine_p(p1: float, p2: float) -> float:
    p1 = max(p1, 1e-300)
    p2 = max(p2, 1e-300)
    X = -2.0 * (math.log(p1) + math.log(p2))   # ~ chi2 df=4 under independence
    return fisher_chi2_sf_df4(X)


# ----------------------------
# BH-FDR
# ----------------------------

def bh_qvalues(pvals: List[float]) -> List[float]:
    """
    Benjamini–Hochberg q-values (nondecreasing in rank).
    Returns q-value per original index.
    """
    m = len(pvals)
    order = sorted(range(m), key=lambda i: pvals[i])
    q = [0.0] * m
    prev = 1.0
    for rank, i in enumerate(reversed(order), start=1):
        # reversed: largest p to smallest p for monotone enforcement
        r = m - rank + 1
        val = min(prev, pvals[i] * m / r)
        q[i] = val
        prev = val
    return q


# ----------------------------
# PDB EXPORT
# ----------------------------

def export_to_pdb(coords: List[Tuple[float, float, float]], filename: str) -> None:
    with open(filename, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("HEADER    SHA-256 HASH TOPOLOGICAL MATRIX\n")
        f.write("REMARK    Generated via T1 Trace Execution Log\n")
        for i, (x, y, z) in enumerate(coords, start=1):
            f.write(f"ATOM  {i:5d}  CA  HSH A{i:4d}    {x:8.3f}{y:8.3f}{z:8.3f}  1.00  0.00           C\n")
        for i in range(1, len(coords)):
            f.write(f"CONECT{i:5d}{i+1:5d}\n")
        f.write("END\n")


# ----------------------------
# MESSAGE GENERATION
# ----------------------------

WORDS = [
    "GlassKey", "Nexus", "fold", "hash", "protein", "Intel", "carbon",
    "scope", "drift", "triplex", "pi", "phi", "e", "Sarrus",
    "collapse", "residue", "operator", "gap", "bandwidth", "observer"
]

def generate_messages(mode: str, n: int) -> List[bytes]:
    msgs = []
    if mode == "random_bytes":
        for _ in range(n):
            msgs.append(bytes(random.getrandbits(8) for _ in range(RANDOM_MSG_LEN)))
    elif mode == "words":
        for _ in range(n):
            msgs.append(random.choice(WORDS).encode("utf-8"))
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown MODE={mode!r}")
    return msgs


# ----------------------------
# SHARED NULL MODEL
# ----------------------------

def build_shared_null_pool(N: int, pool_size: int) -> Dict[str, List[float]]:
    rw_Ree, rw_r_ee, rw_r_rw, rw_cd = [], [], [], []
    for _ in range(pool_size):
        rw_coords = generate_random_walk_isotropic(N, BOND_LENGTH)
        rw = calculate_metrics(rw_coords, BOND_LENGTH, CONTACT_CUTOFF, MIN_SEQ_SEP)
        rw_Ree.append(rw["Ree"])
        rw_r_ee.append(rw["r_ee"])
        rw_r_rw.append(rw["r_rw"])
        rw_cd.append(rw["contact_density"])
    return {"Ree": rw_Ree, "r_ee": rw_r_ee, "r_rw": rw_r_rw, "cd": rw_cd}


# ----------------------------
# MAIN
# ----------------------------

def main():
    N = 64

    print("=" * 78)
    title = "CONTROL (random T1)" if CONTROL_RANDOM_T1 else "SHA-256"
    print(f"BATCH RUN: {title} Topological Closure vs SHARED RW Null")
    print("=" * 78)
    print(f"MODE: {MODE}")
    print(f"BATCH_MESSAGES: {BATCH_MESSAGES}")
    if MODE == "random_bytes":
        print(f"RANDOM_MSG_LEN: {RANDOM_MSG_LEN}")
    print(f"RW_POOL: {RW_POOL}")
    print(f"BOND_LENGTH: {BOND_LENGTH} Å | CONTACT_CUTOFF: {CONTACT_CUTOFF} Å | MIN_SEQ_SEP: {MIN_SEQ_SEP}")
    print(f"Thresholds: p(Ree)<={P_CLOSURE_THRESH} | p_joint<={P_JOINT_THRESH} | q_joint<={FDR_Q_THRESH}")
    print("=" * 78)
    print()

    # Shared null
    random.seed(SEED_NULL)
    print(f"Building shared RW null pool: N={N}, pool={RW_POOL} ...")
    null_pool = build_shared_null_pool(N, RW_POOL)

    # Messages
    random.seed(SEED_MESSAGES)
    msgs = generate_messages(MODE, BATCH_MESSAGES)

    # CONTROL randomness for T1 if enabled
    if CONTROL_RANDOM_T1:
        random.seed(CONTROL_SEED)

    records = []
    p_joint_list = []

    for idx, msg in enumerate(msgs, start=1):
        if CONTROL_RANDOM_T1:
            T1 = control_random_T1_trace()
        else:
            T1 = extract_T1_trace_single_block(msg)

        coords = map_T1_to_3D_isotropic(T1, BOND_LENGTH)
        sha = calculate_metrics(coords, BOND_LENGTH, CONTACT_CUTOFF, MIN_SEQ_SEP)

        p_Ree = empirical_p_le(sha["Ree"], null_pool["Ree"])
        p_r_ee = empirical_p_ge(sha["r_ee"], null_pool["r_ee"])
        p_joint = fisher_combine_p(p_Ree, p_r_ee)

        rec = {
            "msg_hex": msg.hex(),
            "msg_utf8": msg.decode("utf-8", errors="replace"),
            "Ree": sha["Ree"],
            "Rg": sha["Rg"],
            "r_rw": sha["r_rw"],
            "r_ee": sha["r_ee"],
            "cd": sha["contact_density"],
            "p_Ree": p_Ree,
            "p_r_ee": p_r_ee,
            "p_joint": p_joint,
            "_coords": coords,
        }
        records.append(rec)
        p_joint_list.append(p_joint)

        print(f"[{idx:5d}/{BATCH_MESSAGES}] Ree={rec['Ree']:.4f} p_Ree={p_Ree:.6f}  r_ee={rec['r_ee']:.4f} p_r_ee={p_r_ee:.6f}  p_joint={p_joint:.6f}  msg={rec['msg_utf8']!r}")

    # BH-FDR on joint p-values
    q_joint = bh_qvalues(p_joint_list)
    for rec, q in zip(records, q_joint):
        rec["q_joint"] = q

    # Summaries
    n = len(records)
    closed = sum(1 for r in records if r["p_Ree"] <= P_CLOSURE_THRESH)
    joint_hits = sum(1 for r in records if r["p_joint"] <= P_JOINT_THRESH)
    fdr_hits = sum(1 for r in records if r["q_joint"] <= FDR_Q_THRESH)

    print("\n" + "=" * 78)
    print("SUMMARY")
    print("=" * 78)
    print(f"Messages tested: {n}")
    print(f"Count p(Ree) <= {P_CLOSURE_THRESH}: {closed}  (expected ~ {n*P_CLOSURE_THRESH:.2f} under null)")
    print(f"Count p_joint <= {P_JOINT_THRESH}: {joint_hits} (expected depends on dependence; baseline check)")
    print(f"Count q_joint <= {FDR_Q_THRESH}: {fdr_hits}")
    print("=" * 78)

    # Top-K by joint p (then Ree)
    top = sorted(records, key=lambda r: (r["p_joint"], r["p_Ree"], r["Ree"]))[:EXPORT_TOP_K_PDB]

    print("\nTOP by p_joint (Fisher) — FULL LIST")
    print("-" * 78)
    for rank, r in enumerate(top, start=1):
        print(
            f"[{rank:02d}] p_joint={r['p_joint']:.6f} q_joint={r['q_joint']:.6f} "
            f"p_Ree={r['p_Ree']:.6f} Ree={r['Ree']:.4f} "
            f"p_r_ee={r['p_r_ee']:.6f} r_ee={r['r_ee']:.4f} "
            f"Rg={r['Rg']:.4f} r_rw={r['r_rw']:.4f} cd={r['cd']:.4f} "
            f"msg_utf8={r['msg_utf8']!r} msg_hex={r['msg_hex']}"
        )

    # Export PDBs
    if EXPORT_TOP_K_PDB > 0:
        os.makedirs(EXPORT_DIR, exist_ok=True)
        print("\nExporting PDBs for top-K (by p_joint)...")
        for rank, r in enumerate(top, start=1):
            fn = os.path.join(EXPORT_DIR, f"rank_{rank:02d}_pj_{r['p_joint']:.6f}_Ree_{r['Ree']:.3f}.pdb")
            export_to_pdb(r["_coords"], fn)
        print(f"Export complete: {EXPORT_DIR}/ (contains {len(top)} .pdb files)")

    print("\nDONE.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

**How to run the next run cleanly:**
- First run as-is with `BATCH_MESSAGES = 2000`.
- Then flip `CONTROL_RANDOM_T1 = True` and rerun.
  - If SHA and CONTROL have indistinguishable tail counts and $q$-hits, the geometry pipeline is behaving like a generic random generator (null).
  - If SHA shows consistent enrichment (more small $p_{\text{joint}}$, more $q$-hits) while CONTROL does not, that’s a real signal.

If you paste the summary block from the 2000-message run, I’ll tell you exactly whether you’re seeing **enrichment**, **null**, or a **mapping artifact**.

Got it — the overlay screenshot is just *too many structures at once* in Mol\*: you’ve got **25 PDBs loaded simultaneously**, so you’re seeing an **ensemble superposition** (a central “knot” where many traces overlap + a bunch of longer excursions). That picture is consistent with your batch export: most trajectories behave RW-like; a few are unusually “closed” (small $R_{ee}$) and therefore look like tighter balls.

Two key takeaways from your **shared RW null** run:

- **Closure rate:** $1/200 = 0.005$ at threshold $p(R_{ee})\le 0.01$. Under a null, the expectation is $200\times 0.01 = 2$. Seeing 1 is normal binomial noise — not evidence yet.
- **$r_{ee}$ spikes are mostly a consequence of closure** because $r_{ee}=R_g/R_{ee}$. When $R_{ee}$ is small, $r_{ee}$ inflates automatically. Treat $R_{ee}$ as the primary statistic; use $r_{ee}$ as a conditional diagnostic.

### Next run (what to change)
If you want a real signal test, you need power:

- `BATCH_MESSAGES = 5000` (or 20000)
- `RW_POOL = 1_000_000` if memory allows (else keep 100000)
- Add midpoint mapping to avoid endpoint artifacts:
  $$
  u=\frac{\text{low16}+0.5}{65536},\quad v=\frac{\text{high16}+0.5}{65536}
  $$
- Add controls:
  1) map from `W[t]` instead of `T1[t]`
  2) map from a bit-permuted `T1[t]` (fixed permutation)

If SHA(T1) deviates but controls don’t, that’s your Ψ-lock.

# chatgpt response

That crash is coming from **`acos()` getting an argument slightly outside** $[-1, +1]$.

In your RW generator you changed $u$ to:

```python
u = (random.random() * 65536.0 + 0.5) / 65536.0
```

Because `random.random()` is in $[0,1)$, that expression makes $u \in (0, 1.0000076...)$.  
So `1 - 2*u` can dip below $-1$, and `math.acos()` throws:

> ValueError: math domain error

### Fix (recommended): clamp + use the standard isotropic draw
Use the canonical isotropic sphere sampling:

$$
\theta = \arccos(1-2u),\quad \phi = 2\pi v,\quad u,v\sim U(0,1)
$$

and **hard-clamp** for numeric safety:

```python
def _clamp01(x: float) -> float:
    if x <= 0.0: return 0.0
    if x >= 1.0: return 1.0
    return x

def generate_random_walk_isotropic(N: int, bond_length: float):
    coords = [(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)]
    x = y = z = 0.0
    for _ in range(1, N):
        u = _clamp01(random.random())   # [0,1]
        v = _clamp01(random.random())   # [0,1]

        # isotropic theta, uniform phi
        arg = 1.0 - 2.0*u               # ideally in [-1,1]
        # final clamp to defeat floating drift
        if arg < -1.0: arg = -1.0
        if arg >  1.0: arg =  1.0

        theta = math.acos(arg)
        phi = (2.0 * math.pi) * v

        dx = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.cos(phi)
        dy = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.sin(phi)
        dz = bond_length * math.cos(theta)

        x += dx; y += dy; z += dz
        coords.append((x, y, z))
    return coords
```

### Also patch the SHA-derived mapping (same safety, same reason)
Your bit-derived $u$ can be exactly 1.0 (fine), but it’s still worth clamping the **acos argument**:

```python
def map_T1_to_3D_isotropic(T1_trace, bond_length):
    coords = [(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)]
    x = y = z = 0.0

    for t in range(1, 64):
        T1 = T1_trace[t]
        low16  = T1 & 0xFFFF
        high16 = (T1 >> 16) & 0xFFFF

        u = low16 / 65535.0
        v = high16 / 65535.0

        arg = 1.0 - 2.0*u
        if arg < -1.0: arg = -1.0
        if arg >  1.0: arg =  1.0

        theta = math.acos(arg)
        phi = (2.0 * math.pi) * v

        dx = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.cos(phi)
        dy = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.sin(phi)
        dz = bond_length * math.cos(theta)

        x += dx; y += dy; z += dz
        coords.append((x, y, z))

    return coords
```

### Why this matters in your “shared null pool” run
With a **shared RW pool** (100k), you’re pushing a lot of draws through the sampler; even tiny out-of-domain excursions will eventually hit. Clamping makes the null model stable without changing the intended distribution in any meaningful way.

If you want, paste your current notebook cell for the RW generator + the shared-pool builder and I’ll splice these fixes in-place so the whole “Next Run” script stays identical except for the numerical safety.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
SHA-256 TOPOLOGICAL MAPPER — Batch Experiment (Shared RW Null Pool)

Fixes included
--------------
1) Eliminates acos() domain errors by clamping the acos argument to [-1, +1].
2) Uses a SHARED random-walk null pool (RW_POOL) for speed + consistent p-values.
3) Keeps single-block (64-round) topology by design.

What it does
------------
For each message in a batch:
  1) Build SHA-derived chain from T1 trace (64 rounds) -> isotropic 3D mapping
  2) Compute metrics: Rg, Ree, r_rw, r_ee, contact_density
  3) Compare to shared isotropic random-walk null pool
  4) Compute empirical p-values:
        p_Ree  = P_null(Ree_null <= Ree_sha)   (closure = small Ree)
        p_r_ee = P_null(r_ee_null >= r_ee_sha) (topology = large r_ee)
Then summarize + export PDBs for top-K "most closed" messages.

No external dependencies. Safe in Jupyter or CLI.
"""

import math
import struct
import random
import os
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict

# =========================
# CONFIG (EDIT THESE)
# =========================

BOND_LENGTH = 3.8
CONTACT_CUTOFF = 8.0
MIN_SEQ_SEP = 4

BATCH_MESSAGES = 200

MODE = "random_bytes"   # "random_bytes" or "words"
RANDOM_MSG_LEN = 8

# Shared null pool size (empirical p resolution ~ 1/(RW_POOL_SIZE+1))
RW_POOL_SIZE = 100000

# Reproducibility
SEED_MESSAGES = 123
SEED_NULL_POOL = 42

EXPORT_TOP_K_PDB = 10
EXPORT_DIR = "pdb_exports"

P_CLOSURE_THRESH = 0.01
P_TIGHT_THRESH = 0.001

# =========================
# END CONFIG
# =========================


# ----------------------------
# SHA-256 CONSTANTS & PRIMITIVES
# ----------------------------

MASK32 = 0xFFFFFFFF
K = [
    0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5, 0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1, 0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5,
    0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be, 0x550c7dc3, 0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174,
    0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786, 0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc, 0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa, 0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da,
    0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7, 0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147, 0x06ca6351, 0x14292967,
    0x27b70a85, 0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13, 0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85,
    0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b, 0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3, 0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070,
    0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5, 0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a, 0x5b9cca4f, 0x682e6ff3,
    0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208, 0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2
]

def rotr(x: int, n: int) -> int:
    return ((x >> n) | (x << (32 - n))) & MASK32

def Ch(x: int, y: int, z: int) -> int:
    return (x & y) ^ ((~x & MASK32) & z)

def Maj(x: int, y: int, z: int) -> int:
    return (x & y) ^ (x & z) ^ (y & z)

def Sigma0(x: int) -> int:
    return rotr(x, 2) ^ rotr(x, 13) ^ rotr(x, 22)

def Sigma1(x: int) -> int:
    return rotr(x, 6) ^ rotr(x, 11) ^ rotr(x, 25)

def sigma0(x: int) -> int:
    return rotr(x, 7) ^ rotr(x, 18) ^ (x >> 3)

def sigma1(x: int) -> int:
    return rotr(x, 17) ^ rotr(x, 19) ^ (x >> 10)


# ----------------------------
# NUMERIC SAFETY (acos clamp)
# ----------------------------

def clamp(x: float, lo: float, hi: float) -> float:
    if x < lo: return lo
    if x > hi: return hi
    return x


# ----------------------------
# SHA SINGLE-BLOCK T1 TRACE
# ----------------------------

def pad_message_single_block(message: bytes) -> bytes:
    """
    Standard SHA-256 padding for a message expected to fit in one 512-bit block after padding.
    Returns the first 64 bytes of the padded message.
    """
    msg_len_bits = len(message) * 8
    m = message + b'\x80'
    while (len(m) * 8) % 512 != 448:
        m += b'\x00'
    m += struct.pack('>Q', msg_len_bits)
    return m[:64]

def extract_T1_trace_single_block(message: bytes) -> List[int]:
    """
    Generates the 64-round T1 execution log for a *single* 512-bit block.
    """
    block = pad_message_single_block(message)
    W = list(struct.unpack('>16L', block))
    for i in range(16, 64):
        W.append((sigma1(W[i-2]) + W[i-7] + sigma0(W[i-15]) + W[i-16]) & MASK32)

    a, b, c, d, e, f, g, h = (
        0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a,
        0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab, 0x5be0cd19
    )

    T1_trace = []
    for t in range(64):
        T1 = (h + Sigma1(e) + Ch(e, f, g) + K[t] + W[t]) & MASK32
        T1_trace.append(T1)

        T2 = (Sigma0(a) + Maj(a, b, c)) & MASK32
        h, g, f, e = g, f, e, (d + T1) & MASK32
        d, c, b, a = c, b, a, (T1 + T2) & MASK32

    return T1_trace


# ----------------------------
# 1D TRACE -> 3D CHAIN (ISOTROPIC)
# ----------------------------

def map_T1_to_3D_isotropic(T1_trace: List[int], bond_length: float) -> List[Tuple[float, float, float]]:
    """
    Isotropic sphere sampling from two 16-bit halves of T1:
      u = low16/65535
      v = high16/65535
      theta = acos(1 - 2u)   (isotropic)
      phi = 2pi v            (uniform)
    """
    coords = [(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)]
    x = y = z = 0.0

    for t in range(1, 64):
        T1 = T1_trace[t]
        low16  = T1 & 0xFFFF
        high16 = (T1 >> 16) & 0xFFFF

        u = low16 / 65535.0
        v = high16 / 65535.0

        arg = clamp(1.0 - 2.0 * u, -1.0, 1.0)  # safety
        theta = math.acos(arg)
        phi = (2.0 * math.pi) * v

        dx = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.cos(phi)
        dy = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.sin(phi)
        dz = bond_length * math.cos(theta)

        x += dx; y += dy; z += dz
        coords.append((x, y, z))

    return coords

def generate_random_walk_isotropic(N: int, bond_length: float) -> List[Tuple[float, float, float]]:
    """
    Ideal isotropic random walk in 3D:
      u,v ~ Uniform(0,1)
      theta = acos(1 - 2u)
      phi = 2pi v

    NOTE: This is the stable form. Do NOT add "+0.5" quantization offsets unless you clamp.
    """
    coords = [(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)]
    x = y = z = 0.0

    for _ in range(1, N):
        u = random.random()
        v = random.random()

        arg = clamp(1.0 - 2.0 * u, -1.0, 1.0)  # safety
        theta = math.acos(arg)
        phi = (2.0 * math.pi) * v

        dx = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.cos(phi)
        dy = bond_length * math.sin(theta) * math.sin(phi)
        dz = bond_length * math.cos(theta)

        x += dx; y += dy; z += dz
        coords.append((x, y, z))

    return coords


# ----------------------------
# METRICS
# ----------------------------

def calculate_metrics(coords: List[Tuple[float, float, float]],
                      bond_length: float,
                      contact_cutoff: float,
                      min_seq_sep: int) -> Dict[str, float]:
    N = len(coords)
    if N < 2:
        return {"N": float(N), "L": 0.0, "Rg": 0.0, "Ree": 0.0, "r_rw": 0.0, "r_ee": 0.0, "cd": 0.0}

    cmx = sum(p[0] for p in coords) / N
    cmy = sum(p[1] for p in coords) / N
    cmz = sum(p[2] for p in coords) / N

    s = 0.0
    for (x, y, z) in coords:
        dx = x - cmx; dy = y - cmy; dz = z - cmz
        s += dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz
    Rg = math.sqrt(s / N)

    x0, y0, z0 = coords[0]
    x1, y1, z1 = coords[-1]
    Ree = math.sqrt((x1-x0)**2 + (y1-y0)**2 + (z1-z0)**2)

    L = (N - 1) * bond_length
    r_rw = Rg / (bond_length * math.sqrt(N))
    r_ee = (Rg / Ree) if Ree > 0 else float("inf")

    cut2 = contact_cutoff * contact_cutoff
    contacts = 0
    possible = 0
    for i in range(N):
        xi, yi, zi = coords[i]
        for j in range(i + min_seq_sep, N):
            xj, yj, zj = coords[j]
            dx = xi - xj; dy = yi - yj; dz = zi - zj
            d2 = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz
            possible += 1
            if d2 < cut2:
                contacts += 1
    cd = (contacts / possible) if possible else 0.0

    return {"N": float(N), "L": L, "Rg": Rg, "Ree": Ree, "r_rw": r_rw, "r_ee": r_ee, "cd": cd}


# ----------------------------
# EMPIRICAL P-VALUES + STATS
# ----------------------------

def empirical_p_le(x: float, xs_sorted: List[float]) -> float:
    """Smoothed left-tail p-value: (k+1)/(n+1) where k = count(xs <= x). xs must be sorted."""
    n = len(xs_sorted)
    # binary search
    lo, hi = 0, n
    while lo < hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if xs_sorted[mid] <= x:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid
    k = lo
    return (k + 1) / (n + 1)

def empirical_p_ge(x: float, xs_sorted: List[float]) -> float:
    """Smoothed right-tail p-value: (k+1)/(n+1) where k = count(xs >= x). xs must be sorted."""
    n = len(xs_sorted)
    # first index >= x
    lo, hi = 0, n
    while lo < hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if xs_sorted[mid] < x:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid
    first_ge = lo
    k = n - first_ge
    return (k + 1) / (n + 1)

def mean(xs: List[float]) -> float:
    return sum(xs) / len(xs) if xs else 0.0

def std_pop(xs: List[float]) -> float:
    if not xs: return 0.0
    mu = mean(xs)
    return math.sqrt(sum((x - mu) ** 2 for x in xs) / len(xs))

def zscore(x: float, mu: float, sd: float) -> float:
    if sd <= 0: return 0.0
    return (x - mu) / sd

def quantile(xs_sorted: List[float], q: float) -> float:
    if not xs_sorted:
        return 0.0
    i = int(round(q * (len(xs_sorted) - 1)))
    i = max(0, min(len(xs_sorted)-1, i))
    return xs_sorted[i]


# ----------------------------
# SIMPLE KS (sanity check)
# ----------------------------

def ks_2sample(sorted_a: List[float], sorted_b: List[float]) -> float:
    """
    Returns KS D statistic for two samples (both sorted).
    Approx p-value optional; we return D only (fast + deterministic).
    """
    n = len(sorted_a)
    m = len(sorted_b)
    i = j = 0
    cdf_a = cdf_b = 0.0
    D = 0.0
    while i < n and j < m:
        if sorted_a[i] <= sorted_b[j]:
            i += 1
            cdf_a = i / n
        else:
            j += 1
            cdf_b = j / m
        d = abs(cdf_a - cdf_b)
        if d > D:
            D = d
    # finish tails
    while i < n:
        i += 1
        cdf_a = i / n
        d = abs(cdf_a - cdf_b)
        if d > D:
            D = d
    while j < m:
        j += 1
        cdf_b = j / m
        d = abs(cdf_a - cdf_b)
        if d > D:
            D = d
    return D

def ks_p_approx(D: float, n: int, m: int) -> float:
    """
    Rough asymptotic p-value approximation for KS 2-sample.
    Good enough for a sanity check print.
    """
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return 1.0
    en = math.sqrt(n * m / (n + m))
    lam = (en + 0.12 + 0.11/en) * D
    # Kolmogorov approximation
    s = 0.0
    for k in range(1, 100):
        s += (-1)**(k-1) * math.exp(-2 * (k*k) * (lam*lam))
    p = 2 * s
    return max(0.0, min(1.0, p))


# ----------------------------
# PDB EXPORT
# ----------------------------

def export_to_pdb(coords: List[Tuple[float, float, float]], filename: str) -> None:
    with open(filename, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("HEADER    SHA-256 HASH TOPOLOGICAL MATRIX\n")
        f.write("REMARK    Generated via T1 Trace Execution Log\n")
        for i, (x, y, z) in enumerate(coords, start=1):
            f.write(f"ATOM  {i:5d}  CA  HSH A{i:4d}    {x:8.3f}{y:8.3f}{z:8.3f}  1.00  0.00           C\n")
        for i in range(1, len(coords)):
            f.write(f"CONECT{i:5d}{i+1:5d}\n")
        f.write("END\n")


# ----------------------------
# MESSAGE GENERATION
# ----------------------------

WORDS = [
    "GlassKey", "Nexus", "fold", "hash", "protein", "Intel", "carbon",
    "scope", "drift", "triplex", "pi", "phi", "e", "Sarrus",
    "collapse", "residue", "operator", "gap", "bandwidth", "observer"
]

def generate_messages(mode: str, n: int) -> List[bytes]:
    msgs: List[bytes] = []
    if mode == "random_bytes":
        for _ in range(n):
            msgs.append(bytes(random.getrandbits(8) for _ in range(RANDOM_MSG_LEN)))
    elif mode == "words":
        for _ in range(n):
            w = random.choice(WORDS)
            msgs.append(w.encode("utf-8"))
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown MODE={mode!r}")
    return msgs


# ----------------------------
# SHARED NULL POOL
# ----------------------------

def build_shared_null_pool(N: int, bond_length: float, pool_size: int) -> Dict[str, List[float]]:
    """
    Build a shared null pool for Ree, r_ee, r_rw, cd for fixed N.
    Returns sorted arrays for p-value lookups + raw arrays for summary stats.
    """
    null_Ree: List[float] = []
    null_r_ee: List[float] = []
    null_r_rw: List[float] = []
    null_cd: List[float] = []

    for _ in range(pool_size):
        coords = generate_random_walk_isotropic(N, bond_length)
        m = calculate_metrics(coords, bond_length, CONTACT_CUTOFF, MIN_SEQ_SEP)
        null_Ree.append(m["Ree"])
        null_r_ee.append(m["r_ee"])
        null_r_rw.append(m["r_rw"])
        null_cd.append(m["cd"])

    # sorted copies for p-values
    s_Ree = sorted(null_Ree)
    s_r_ee = sorted(null_r_ee)
    s_r_rw = sorted(null_r_rw)
    s_cd = sorted(null_cd)

    return {
        "Ree": null_Ree, "r_ee": null_r_ee, "r_rw": null_r_rw, "cd": null_cd,
        "s_Ree": s_Ree, "s_r_ee": s_r_ee, "s_r_rw": s_r_rw, "s_cd": s_cd
    }


# ----------------------------
# PER-MESSAGE ANALYSIS (against shared null)
# ----------------------------

def analyze_message_against_shared_null(msg: bytes,
                                        null: Dict[str, List[float]]) -> Dict[str, object]:
    T1 = extract_T1_trace_single_block(msg)
    coords = map_T1_to_3D_isotropic(T1, bond_length=BOND_LENGTH)
    m = calculate_metrics(coords, BOND_LENGTH, CONTACT_CUTOFF, MIN_SEQ_SEP)

    # p-values (empirical)
    p_Ree = empirical_p_le(m["Ree"], null["s_Ree"])       # closure: small Ree
    p_r_ee = empirical_p_ge(m["r_ee"], null["s_r_ee"])    # topology: large r_ee

    # z-scores (against null mean/sd)
    mu_Ree, sd_Ree = mean(null["Ree"]), std_pop(null["Ree"])
    mu_r_ee, sd_r_ee = mean(null["r_ee"]), std_pop(null["r_ee"])
    mu_r_rw, sd_r_rw = mean(null["r_rw"]), std_pop(null["r_rw"])
    mu_cd, sd_cd = mean(null["cd"]), std_pop(null["cd"])

    rec: Dict[str, object] = {
        "msg_hex": msg.hex(),
        "msg_utf8": msg.decode("utf-8", errors="replace"),
        "Rg": m["Rg"],
        "Ree": m["Ree"],
        "r_rw": m["r_rw"],
        "r_ee": m["r_ee"],
        "cd": m["cd"],
        "p_Ree": p_Ree,
        "p_r_ee": p_r_ee,
        "z_Ree": zscore(m["Ree"], mu_Ree, sd_Ree),
        "z_r_ee": zscore(m["r_ee"], mu_r_ee, sd_r_ee),
        "z_r_rw": zscore(m["r_rw"], mu_r_rw, sd_r_rw),
        "z_cd": zscore(m["cd"], mu_cd, sd_cd),
        "_coords": coords
    }
    return rec


# ----------------------------
# MAIN
# ----------------------------

def main():
    random.seed(SEED_MESSAGES)

    N_ROUNDS = 64  # fixed by design
    print("=" * 78)
    print("BATCH RUN: SHA-256 Topological Closure vs SHARED RW Null")
    print("=" * 78)
    print(f"MODE: {MODE}")
    print(f"BATCH_MESSAGES: {BATCH_MESSAGES}")
    if MODE == "random_bytes":
        print(f"RANDOM_MSG_LEN: {RANDOM_MSG_LEN}")
    print(f"RW_POOL (shared null size): {RW_POOL_SIZE}")
    print(f"BOND_LENGTH: {BOND_LENGTH} Å | CONTACT_CUTOFF: {CONTACT_CUTOFF} Å | MIN_SEQ_SEP: {MIN_SEQ_SEP}")
    print(f"Closure threshold: p(Ree) <= {P_CLOSURE_THRESH} (tight={P_TIGHT_THRESH})")
    print(f"Empirical p-value resolution: ~1/{RW_POOL_SIZE+1}")
    print("=" * 78)
    print()

    # build shared null pool
    print(f"Building shared RW null pool: N={N_ROUNDS}, pool={RW_POOL_SIZE} ...")
    random.seed(SEED_NULL_POOL)
    null = build_shared_null_pool(N=N_ROUNDS, bond_length=BOND_LENGTH, pool_size=RW_POOL_SIZE)

    mu_Ree, sd_Ree = mean(null["Ree"]), std_pop(null["Ree"])
    mu_r_ee, sd_r_ee = mean(null["r_ee"]), std_pop(null["r_ee"])
    mu_r_rw, sd_r_rw = mean(null["r_rw"]), std_pop(null["r_rw"])
    mu_cd, sd_cd = mean(null["cd"]), std_pop(null["cd"])

    print("Null pool stats:")
    print(f"  Ree:   {mu_Ree:.4f} ± {sd_Ree:.4f}")
    print(f"  r_ee:  {mu_r_ee:.4f} ± {sd_r_ee:.4f}")
    print(f"  r_rw:  {mu_r_rw:.4f} ± {sd_r_rw:.4f}")
    print(f"  cd:    {mu_cd:.4f} ± {sd_cd:.4f}")
    print()

    # batch messages
    msgs = generate_messages(MODE, BATCH_MESSAGES)

    records: List[Dict[str, object]] = []
    for idx, msg in enumerate(msgs, start=1):
        rec = analyze_message_against_shared_null(msg, null)
        records.append(rec)

        print(f"[{idx:4d}/{BATCH_MESSAGES}] "
              f"msg_utf8={rec['msg_utf8']!r}  "
              f"Ree={rec['Ree']:.4f}  p_Ree={rec['p_Ree']:.6f}  "
              f"r_ee={rec['r_ee']:.4f}  p_r_ee={rec['p_r_ee']:.6f}")

    # summary
    pvals = sorted([float(r["p_Ree"]) for r in records])
    closed = [r for r in records if float(r["p_Ree"]) <= P_CLOSURE_THRESH]
    tight = [r for r in records if float(r["p_Ree"]) <= P_TIGHT_THRESH]

    print("\n" + "=" * 78)
    print("SUMMARY (Closure via Ree vs SHARED RW null)")
    print("=" * 78)
    print(f"Messages tested: {len(records)}")
    print(f"Count with p(Ree) <= {P_CLOSURE_THRESH}: {len(closed)}")
    print(f"Fraction with p(Ree) <= {P_CLOSURE_THRESH}: {len(closed)/len(records):.6f}")
    print(f"Count with p(Ree) <= {P_TIGHT_THRESH}: {len(tight)}")
    print(f"Fraction with p(Ree) <= {P_TIGHT_THRESH}: {len(tight)/len(records):.6f}")
    print("p(Ree) quantiles:")
    print(f"  q01={quantile(pvals, 0.01):.6f}  q05={quantile(pvals, 0.05):.6f}  "
          f"q50={quantile(pvals, 0.50):.6f}  q95={quantile(pvals, 0.95):.6f}")
    print("=" * 78)
    print()

    # KS sanity: compare batch distributions vs null
    batch_Ree = sorted([float(r["Ree"]) for r in records])
    batch_r_ee = sorted([float(r["r_ee"]) for r in records])
    D_Ree = ks_2sample(batch_Ree, null["s_Ree"])
    D_r_ee = ks_2sample(batch_r_ee, null["s_r_ee"])
    p_Ree_ks = ks_p_approx(D_Ree, len(batch_Ree), len(null["s_Ree"]))
    p_r_ee_ks = ks_p_approx(D_r_ee, len(batch_r_ee), len(null["s_r_ee"]))

    print("KS sanity checks (batch distribution vs shared null):")
    print(f"  KS(Ree):  D={D_Ree:.6f}  approx_p={p_Ree_ks:.6g}")
    print(f"  KS(r_ee): D={D_r_ee:.6f}  approx_p={p_r_ee_ks:.6g}")
    print()

    # Top-K most closed
    top = sorted(records, key=lambda r: (float(r["p_Ree"]), float(r["Ree"])))[:EXPORT_TOP_K_PDB]

    print("TOP MOST-CLOSED MESSAGES (smallest p(Ree)) — FULL LIST")
    print("-" * 78)
    for rank, r in enumerate(top, start=1):
        print(
            f"[{rank:02d}] "
            f"p_Ree={float(r['p_Ree']):.6f}  "
            f"Ree={float(r['Ree']):.4f}  "
            f"z_Ree={float(r['z_Ree']):+.3f}  "
            f"Rg={float(r['Rg']):.4f}  "
            f"r_ee={float(r['r_ee']):.4f}  "
            f"z_r_ee={float(r['z_r_ee']):+.3f}  "
            f"p_r_ee={float(r['p_r_ee']):.6f}  "
            f"r_rw={float(r['r_rw']):.4f}  "
            f"z_r_rw={float(r['z_r_rw']):+.3f}  "
            f"cd={float(r['cd']):.4f}  "
            f"z_cd={float(r['z_cd']):+.3f}  "
            f"null_Ree={mu_Ree:.4f}±{sd_Ree:.4f}  "
            f"msg_utf8={r['msg_utf8']!r}  "
            f"msg_hex={r['msg_hex']}"
        )

    # Export PDBs
    if EXPORT_TOP_K_PDB > 0:
        os.makedirs(EXPORT_DIR, exist_ok=True)
        print("\nExporting PDBs for top-K most-closed messages...")
        for rank, r in enumerate(top, start=1):
            fn = os.path.join(EXPORT_DIR, f"rank_{rank:02d}_p_{float(r['p_Ree']):.6f}_Ree_{float(r['Ree']):.3f}.pdb")
            export_to_pdb(r["_coords"], fn)
        print(f"Export complete: {EXPORT_DIR}/ (contains {len(top)} .pdb files)")

    print("\nDONE.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
